1595 — smith front squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1595 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A front squat with the bar guided by the smith machine. It trains the quads with an upright torso.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 1595.json#/name

Arms Hands

Set the bar at shoulder height and rest it on the front of your shoulders with elbows up.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1595.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Use a comfortable hip-width stance with soft knees unless the source specifies a single-leg, split or wider stance; keep the documented support foot grounded.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1595.json#/name

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set the bar at shoulder height and rest it on the front of your shoulders with elbows up. 1595.json#/instructions/0
  2. Unrack it and step your feet slightly forward. 1595.json#/instructions/1
  3. Squat down until your thighs are about parallel to the floor. 1595.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your elbows high and your chest up. 1595.json#/instructions/3
  5. Press through your heels to stand and rerack when done. 1595.json#/instructions/4

1596 — smith squat to bench

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1596 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A smith machine squat down to a bench behind you. The bench gives a consistent depth target.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Place a bench behind you under the smith bar. Rest the bar on your upper back and unrack it. Sit your hips back and lower until you touch the bench lightly.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1596.json#/instructions/0, 1596.json#/instructions/1, 1596.json#/instructions/2

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

1597 — smith stiff legged deadlift

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1597 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A stiff-legged deadlift using the smith machine bar. The fixed path lets you focus on feeling the hamstrings work.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand close to the smith bar with feet hip-width apart and grip the bar overhand.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand close to the smith bar with feet hip-width apart and grip the bar overhand. 1597.json#/instructions/0
  2. Unrack it and keep your knees nearly straight. 1597.json#/instructions/1
  3. Push your hips back and slide the bar down your legs. 1597.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower until you feel a deep stretch in your hamstrings. 1597.json#/instructions/3
  5. Squeeze your glutes to stand tall. 1597.json#/instructions/4

1598 — smith zercher squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1598 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A squat holding the bar in the crooks of your elbows on the smith machine. It trains the legs while taxing the core and upper back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

1599 — standing hamstring and calf stretch with strap

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1599 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Standing with a strap around your foot, you pull your toes toward you to stretch the hamstring and calf.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand upright with your feet shoulder-width apart. Hold the strap with both hands and place it around the ball of your foot.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1599.json#/instructions/0, 1599.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold the strap with both hands and place it around the ball of your foot.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1599.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Stand upright with your feet shoulder-width apart. Hold the strap with both hands and place it around the ball of your foot.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1599.json#/instructions/0, 1599.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Stretching strap. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1599.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand upright with your feet shoulder-width apart. 1599.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the strap with both hands and place it around the ball of your foot. 1599.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your leg straight and slowly lean forward from your hips, keeping your back straight. 1599.json#/instructions/2
  4. Feel the stretch in your hamstring and calf muscles. 1599.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold the stretch for 20-30 seconds. 1599.json#/instructions/4
  6. Release the stretch and repeat with the other leg. 1599.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1599.json#/instructions/6

1600 — standing long jump

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1600 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A jump for distance from a standing start. It builds explosive power in the hips and legs.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with feet shoulder-width apart at a clear starting line.

Arms Hands

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with feet shoulder-width apart at a clear starting line. 1600.json#/instructions/0
  2. Swing your arms back and bend your knees. 1600.json#/instructions/1

  5. Reset and repeat. 1600.json#/instructions/4

1601 — standing straight leg hamstring contract relax on exercise ball

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1601 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A hamstring stretch with the heel on a stability ball, using a contract and relax method. It helps you ease deeper into the stretch.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand and rest one heel on a stability ball in front of you with your knee straight.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1601.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 1601.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand and rest one heel on a stability ball in front of you with your knee straight.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1601.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Swiss/stability ball. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1601.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand and rest one heel on a stability ball in front of you with your knee straight. 1601.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hinge forward at the hips until you feel a stretch in the back of the thigh. 1601.json#/instructions/1
  3. Gently press your heel into the ball for about 5 seconds, then relax. 1601.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lean a little deeper into the stretch. 1601.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold for 20-30 seconds, then switch legs. 1601.json#/instructions/4

1658 — dumbbell lunge with bicep curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1658 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A lunge paired with a bicep curl. It trains the biceps, quads and glutes together.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand with your palms facing forward.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1658.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand with your palms facing forward.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1658.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand with your palms facing forward.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1658.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1658.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand with your palms facing forward. 1658.json#/instructions/0
  2. Take a step forward with your right foot, lowering your body into a lunge position. Your right knee should be bent at a 90-degree angle and your left knee should be hovering just above the ground. 1658.json#/instructions/1
  3. As you lunge forward, simultaneously curl the dumbbells towards your shoulders, keeping your elbows close to your body. 1658.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the bottom of the lunge, then push through your right heel to return to the starting position, lowering the dumbbells back down to your sides. 1658.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat the lunge and bicep curl on the opposite side, stepping forward with your left foot. 1658.json#/instructions/4
  6. Continue alternating sides for the desired number of repetitions. 1658.json#/instructions/5
Extracted position and contact cues

Starting Position

  • Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand with your palms facing forward. Step 1; 1658.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

  • Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand with your palms facing forward. Step 1; 1658.json#/instructions/0
  • As you lunge forward, simultaneously curl the dumbbells towards your shoulders, keeping your elbows close to your body. Step 3; 1658.json#/instructions/2

Legs Feet

  • Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell in each hand with your palms facing forward. Step 1; 1658.json#/instructions/0
  • Take a step forward with your right foot, lowering your body into a lunge position. Your right knee should be bent at a 90-degree angle and your left knee should be hovering just above the ground. Step 2; 1658.json#/instructions/1
  • Pause for a moment at the bottom of the lunge, then push through your right heel to return to the starting position, lowering the dumbbells back down to your sides. Step 4; 1658.json#/instructions/3
  • Repeat the lunge and bicep curl on the opposite side, stepping forward with your left foot. Step 5; 1658.json#/instructions/4
